<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

START                                                             END
                                                                         
$\Huge \boxed {...}$  Subtract 28 $\Huge \boxed {...}$  Add 19 $\Huge \boxed {63}$  

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let first missing number $= a$ 

Let second missing number $= b$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
b + 19      &=  63  \\\\
b           &=  63 - 19  \\\\
b           &=  44 \\\\
a-28        &=  44   \\\\
a           &=  44 + 28 \\\\
a           &=  72
\end{aligned}
$


</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$72, 44$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

%empty%

</div>
</div>

%empty%

\end{aligned}
$

</div>

\end{aligned}

<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

Goodison Park is a further $1200\,m$ from my house. How many **kilometres** is Goodison Park from my house?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1200 \ \text{metres} \\\\
&= \dfrac{1200}{1000}  \ \text{kilometres} \\\\
&= 1.2 \ \text{kilometres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$1.2 \ \text{km}$

</div>
</div>

&= 1.2 \ \text{kilometres}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

</li>
<li>
<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

A kilometre is $\dfrac {5} {8}$  of a mile. How many miles away from Goodison Park do I live?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Distance}     &= 1.2 \ \text{km} \\\\
                    &= 1.2 \times \dfrac {5} {8} \ \text{mile} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 1.2} {8} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {6} {8} \\\\
                    &= 0.75 \ \text{miles}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$0.75 \ \text{miles}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

                    &= 1.2 \times \dfrac {5} {8} \ \text{mile} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {5 \times 1.2} {8} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac {6} {8} \\\\
                    &= 0.75 \ \text{miles}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='answercodeblock'>

&=  70 \ \text {mm} \\\\
&=  \dfrac{70}{10} \ \text {centimetres} \\\\
&=  \dfrac{7\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}} \\\\
&=  7 \ \text {centimetres}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>

&=  \dfrac{7\cancel{0}}{1\cancel{0}} \\\\

\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E00F44C5-0502-46F6-927E-4C50E44DA4BF'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

$18 - 17 + 16 - 15 + 14 - 13$ 

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 18 - 17 + 16 - 15 + 14 - 13 \\\\
&=  (18 - 17) + (16 - 15) + (14 - 13) \\\\
&= 1 + 1 + 1 \\\\
&= 3
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$3$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 18 - 17 + 16 - 15 + 14 - 13 \\\\
&=  (18 - 17) + (16 - 15) + (14 - 13) \\\\
&= 1 + 1 + 1 \\\\
&= 3
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-88D05932-EA04-4EA0-8EB7-90462AA68E6E'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2 \\\\
&= 2.4 \times 5 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2.4 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 1.2 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 1.2 \times 5}{\cancel {2}} \\\\  
&= 1.2 \times 5 \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2.4 \times (3.7 +  1.3) \div 2 \\\\
&= 2.4 \times 5 \div 2 \\\\
&= \dfrac{2.4 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 1.2 \times 5}{2} \\\\
&= \dfrac{\cancel {2} \times 1.2 \times 5}{\cancel {2}} \\\\  
&= 1.2 \times 5 \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$6$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7B4003E7-EBF5-425C-9D44-B0E047531A36'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $1.1 \times (1.2 + 1.3) \times 2$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.1 \times (1.2 + 1.3) \times 2 \\\\
&= 1.1 \times 2.5 \times 2 \\\\
&= 1.1 \times 5 \\\\
&= 5.5
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$5.5$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 1.1 \times (1.2 + 1.3) \times 2 \\\\
&= 1.1 \times 2.5 \times 2 \\\\
&= 1.1 \times 5 \\\\
&= 5.5
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$5.5$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-FB54FC28-0CE7-4805-A51B-22A1806749EC'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Put a circle around all the numbers that are between $2.2$ and $2.6$

$2 \dfrac{1}{2} \quad 2 \dfrac{1}{7} \quad 2 \dfrac{6}{11} \quad 2 \dfrac{2}{5} \quad 2 \dfrac{1}{10}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let's convert all the fractions into decimals.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {1} {2}, \quad 2 \dfrac {1} {7}, \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11}, \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}, \quad 2 \dfrac {1} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2}, \quad \dfrac {15} {7}, \quad \dfrac {28} {11}, \quad \dfrac {12} {5}, \quad \dfrac {21} {10} \\\\
&= 2.5 \quad  2.14,  \quad 2.55, \quad 2.4, \quad 2.1
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Numbers in between}   &= 2.5, \quad 2.55, \quad 2.4 \\\\
                            &= 2 \dfrac {1} {2}, \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11}, \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$2 \dfrac {1} {2} \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11} \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>Let's convert all the fractions into decimals.

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 2 \dfrac {1} {2}, \quad 2 \dfrac {1} {7}, \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11}, \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}, \quad 2 \dfrac {1} {10} \\\\
&= \dfrac {5} {2}, \quad \dfrac {15} {7}, \quad \dfrac {28} {11}, \quad \dfrac {12} {5}, \quad \dfrac {21} {10} \\\\
&= 2.5 \quad  2.14,  \quad 2.55, \quad 2.4, \quad 2.1
\end{aligned}
$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Numbers in between}   &= 2.5, \quad 2.55, \quad 2.4 \\\\
                            &= 2 \dfrac {1} {2}, \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11}, \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$2 \dfrac {1} {2} \quad 2 \dfrac {6} {11} \quad 2 \dfrac {2} {5}$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$6 \times 8 = 4 \times \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing value $ = a $

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned} 
6 \times 8 &= 4 \times a\\\\
a &= \dfrac {6 \times 8}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times 4}{4} \\\\
a &= \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times \cancel {4}}{\cancel {4}} \\\\
a &= 6\times 2\\\\
a &= 12
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$12$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

a &= \dfrac {6 \times 2 \times \cancel {4}}{\cancel {4}} \\\\

a &= 12
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class='question_envelope rag_not_found rag_not_found subquestion'>
<div class='question subquestion'>

$12 \times 75 = 10 \times \ldots$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

Let the missing value $ = a $

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
12 \times 75 &= 10 \times a \\\\
a &=\dfrac{12\times 75}{10} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{6 \times 2 \times 5 \times 15}{2 \times 5} \\\\
a &= \dfrac{6 \times \cancel {2} \times \cancel {5} \times 15}{\cancel {2} \times \cancel {5}} \\\\
a &= 6 \times 15 \\\\
a &= 90
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$90$

</div>

\end{aligned}
$

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-E16D6325-36FF-464C-8198-472884A6720D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Calculate $(2.3 + 3.7) \div 9$. Leave your answer as a simplified fraction.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

ABBR: Using BIDMAS
,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (2.3 + 3.7) \div 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac{6}{9} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 3}{3 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3}}{3 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\dfrac{2}{3}$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>rtq_abbr_md_bidmas,

$
\begin{aligned}
&= (2.3 + 3.7) \div 9 \\\\
&= \dfrac{6}{9} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times 3}{3 \times 3} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2 \times \cancel{3}}{3 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
&= \dfrac{2}{3}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\dfrac{2}{3}$
</code></pre>
</div>
